TL;DR: AgentX 是一个在生产环境中部署的多智能体系统，通过四个紧密耦合的阶段（构思、开发、评估、进化）实现推荐算法的自动化迭代闭环，使系统具备自我进化的能力，大幅提升实验效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐算法迭代从手工操作向工业化转型的过程中，存在结构性瓶颈：创意到上线依赖人工完成假设生成、代码修改、A/B实验和结果归因，导致创新效率与人员数量线性相关，而非与证据、计算资源和实验知识积累呈复合增长。

Method: AgentX 采用多智能体架构，包含四个闭环阶段：(1) Brainstorm Agent 综合历史实验、系统架构、数据分析和外部研究生成可执行的提案；(2) Developing Agent 基于代码仓库进行代码生成和多维可靠性验证；(3) Evaluation Agent 通过安全在线部署和护栏否决的A/B评估，将成败转化为结构化知识；(4) SGPO Harness Evolution 层将执行轨迹蒸馏为语义梯度更新，持续优化智能体自身。

Result: 系统能够自主生成、实现、评估并从推荐实验中学习，其规模和速度远超人工工作流，实现了从自动化到自我进化的跨越。

Conclusion: AgentX 从根本上重构了推荐算法迭代的生产函数，打破了人力瓶颈，使创新从线性增长转变为复合增长模式。

Abstract: Recommendation algorithm iteration is moving from an artisanal, engineer-bound process toward an industrialized research loop, but this transition remains blocked by a structural execution bottleneck: the idea-to-launch cycle still depends on human engineers to generate hypotheses, modify production code, launch A/B experiments, and attribute online results. Innovation therefore scales linearly with headcount rather than compounding with evidence, compute, and accumulated experimental knowledge. We present AgentX, a production-deployed multi-agent system that fundamentally restructures this production function. AgentX operates as a self-evolving development engine: it autonomously generates, implements, evaluates, and learns from recommendation experiments at a scale and pace that no manual workflow can sustain.
  The system orchestrates four tightly coupled stages in a closed loop. A Brainstorm Agent synthesizes evidence from historical experiments, system architecture, data analysis, and external research into ranked, executable proposals. A Developing Agent translates each proposal into production-ready code through repository-grounded generation and multi-dimensional reliability verification. An Evaluation Agent conducts safe online rollout with guardrail-vetoed A/B judgment, converting both successes and failures into structured knowledge assets. A Harness Evolution layer (SGPO) then distills execution trajectories into semantic-gradient updates that continuously sharpen the agents themselves -- making the system not merely automated, but self-improving.

</details>


### [147] [TAVR-VLM: Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding for Hallucination-Resistant Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26874)
*Zhixiang Lu,Xiwei Liu,Sifan Song,Changkai Ji,Anh Nguyen,Jionglong Su,Imran Razzak,Jinfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TAVR-VLM通过风险条件因果注意力（R-CGA）框架，在TAVR手术规划中建立"风险→区域→词"的结构化约束路径，显著降低多模态大模型的诊断幻觉（降至8.1%），在1482例患者队列中实现AUROC 0.896、CIDEr 0.936的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在TAVR手术规划中存在严重的诊断幻觉问题，生成的文本缺乏解剖学依据，无法满足高风险医疗场景对可解释性和可靠性的要求。

Method: 提出R-CGA机制，将多模态输入压缩为因果风险瓶颈，生成全局风险掩码；在自回归生成中通过支持投影因果一致性目标约束token级定位，实现"风险→区域→词"的结构化解耦。

Result: 在M³TAVR数据集（1482例患者）上，AUROC达0.896，CIDEr提升至0.936，幻觉率大幅降至8.1%。

Conclusion: TAVR-VLM通过结构化因果约束有效抑制了高风险医疗场景中的诊断幻觉，为循证手术AI提供了更可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Transcatheter Aortic Valve Replacement (TAVR) planning requires meticulous multimodal reasoning. However, adapting Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to this high-stakes domain is severely impeded by diagnostic hallucinations, where generated text lacks anatomical grounding. To address this, TAVR-VLM is introduced: a novel framework featuring Risk-Conditioned Causal Grounding Attention (R-CGA) that instantiates a model-internal ``Risk $\rightarrow$ Region $\rightarrow$ Word'' structural grounding pathway. R-CGA compresses multimodal inputs into a causal risk bottleneck, purifying dense visual features into a global risk mask. During autoregressive generation, a support-projected causal consistency objective constrains token-level grounding within the risk-defined support mask. Evaluated on $\text{M}^3\text{TAVR}$, a comprehensive 1,482-patient cohort, TAVR-VLM establishes a new state-of-the-art. It achieves an AUROC of 0.896, boosts CIDEr to 0.936, and drastically reduces the hallucination rate to 8.1\%, thereby improving interpretability for evidence-based surgical AI.

</details>


### [148] [A Pipeline for Generating Longitudinal Synthetic Clinical Notes Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26879)
*William Poulett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套合成临床笔记的生成流水线和数据集，通过结合结构化患者生成、半结构化病程模拟和大语言模型生成非结构化临床笔记，在避免真实患者数据隐私风险的同时，支持临床AI系统的开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域临床文档涉及高度敏感的真实患者数据，由于隐私限制，研究者难以获取充足数据用于AI系统的开发和评估。合成数据成为解决这一困境的关键途径。

Method: 采用模块化流水线：(1) 结构化患者生成；(2) 半结构化患者病程模拟；(3) 使用大语言模型生成非结构化临床笔记。通过LLM验证和增强机制确保内部一致性、真实性、多样性和写作风格变化。

Result: 生成了包含70名合成患者的数据集，每位患者有20-50份临床笔记，覆盖完整住院过程。数据集提供多级验证版本，用户可根据使用场景在真实性和可扩展性之间权衡。

Conclusion: 该合成数据集能够支持临床AI系统的开发、测试和评估（如摘要工具、编码模型、决策支持系统），无需依赖真实患者数据，有效规避隐私风险。

Abstract: Synthetic data is increasingly used to enable the development and evaluation of AI systems in domains where access to real-world data is restricted. In healthcare, clinical documentation presents particular challenges due to its sensitivity. This work introduces a synthetic clinical notes pipeline and dataset designed to support the development of clinical AI tools while avoiding the privacy risks associated with real patient data. The dataset is generated using a modular pipeline that combines structured patient generation, semi-structured patient journey simulation, and unstructured clinical note generation using large language models. The pipeline is designed to prioritise internal consistency across longitudinal patient records, while also capturing variation in writing style, note structure, and clinical detail. Additional mechanisms, including LLM-based validation and augmentation steps, are used to improve faithfulness, realism, and diversity of the generated notes. We release a dataset of 70 synthetic patients, each associated with 20-50 clinical notes spanning a full hospital journey. The dataset is provided at multiple levels of validation, enabling users to balance realism and scalability depending on their use case. This dataset supports the development, testing, and evaluation of clinical AI systems, including summarisation tools, coding models, and decision support systems, without reliance on real patient data.

</details>


### [149] [Generative Retrieval via Diffusion Transformer with Metric-Ordered Sequence Training and Hybrid-Policy Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26899)
*Chenghao Liu,Yu Zhang,Zhongtao Jiang,Kun Xu,Zhenwei An,Renzhi Wang,Zhao Wang,Jiachen Zhang,Yuxiao Zhang,Kun Xu,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-DiT+HPPO框架，通过度量有序连续预训练、尾部质心微调和混合偏好优化，解决生成式检索中“保持模式+提升属性”的冲突目标，在多个属性域上显著提升了交集指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入检索中，给定一个种子集，目标不仅是返回最相似的项，而是需要找到既满足目标属性又保持种子集所表达的细粒度模式的新项。这两个目标相互冲突：平均种子保持模式但属性区域低，而全局属性检索会漂移到无关模式。现有方法无法同时满足这两个要求。

Method: 提出了MO-DiT+HPPO分阶段框架：1) 原始序列预训练；2) 多领域度量有序连续预训练（将稀疏的在线检索标签转化为从低到高预测属性密度的按模式内轨迹，跨域学习度量改进方向）；3) 尾部质心微调；4) HPPO混合偏好优化（通过标注混合候选池的在线交集指标，应用参考锚定偏好优化）。其中Pareto对过滤器只保留不降低模式纯度的优胜对。

Result: 在四个属性域的项保留和模式保留协议下，度量有序DiT相比预训练生成式检索器提升了交集指标，HPPO进一步提升了该指标，在8个域-拆分组合中有7个取得显著增益，最难的拆分上为边际持平。

Conclusion: 度量预测器验证、顺序消融、CPT/SFT比较和候选策略消融等实验揭示了各组件带来的增益来源。该方法有效解决了模式保持与属性提升之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval ranks items by their similarity to a query in a shared vector space and usually aims to return the highest-scoring items. In many production settings this is not what is wanted: given a seed set that expresses a fine-grained pattern, one needs more items that both satisfy a target attribute and stay within that pattern. We formalize this as pattern-preserving attribute retrieval. The two goals pull against each other: averaging the seeds preserves the pattern but stays in a low-attribute region, while global attribute retrieval drifts to unrelated patterns. We approach the task with continuous generative retrieval, where a model reads a sequence of item embeddings and generates query embeddings for nearest-neighbor search. We propose MO-DiT+HPPO, a staged framework with raw-sequence pretraining, multi-domain metric-ordered continuation pretraining, tail-centroid fine-tuning, and HPPO. Metric-ordered training turns sparse online retrieval labels into in-pattern trajectories ordered from low to high predicted attribute density, teaching one model the metric-improvement direction across domains. HPPO aligns the generated query distribution with the true online objective by labeling a hybrid candidate pool with the online intersection metric and applying reference-anchored preference optimization. A Pareto pair filter keeps only winner pairs that do not lower same-pattern purity, raising the attribute metric without sacrificing the pattern. Across four attribute domains under item- and pattern-holdout protocols, metric-ordered DiT improves the intersection metric over a pretrained generative retriever, and HPPO improves it further, with significant gains on seven of eight domain-split cells and a marginal tie on the hardest split. Metric-predictor validation, order ablations, CPT/SFT comparisons, and a candidate-policy ablation show where the gains come from.

</details>


### [150] [Learning to Recover Task Experts from a Multi-Task Merged Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26902)
*Jinwook Jung,Taegyu Kim,Kumju Jo,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTeX框架，通过预测参数偏移来撤销多任务模型合并中的参数干扰，从单个合并检查点恢复专家性能；同时利用SVD子空间签名实现无路由的任务识别，在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的单独专家性能，并显著提升对未见任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务模型合并存在参数干扰问题，静态合并方法效果差，而动态合并方法需要存储和加载冗余专家组件，推理成本高。

Method: 1) 将参数干扰建模为仿射变换并近似为加性偏移；2) 提出ReTeX框架预测偏移量以撤销干扰；3) 基于SVD子空间签名设计无路由任务标识器，通过最小投影残差识别任务。

Result: 在视觉和NLP领域恢复超过95%的单独专家性能，显著提升对未见任务的泛化能力；参数偏移预测导致对OOD任务的适应性知识插值。

Conclusion: ReTeX通过参数偏移预测有效解决了多任务合并中的干扰问题，且无需冗余专家存储，同时展现出对未见任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging aims to consolidate several task-specific experts into a unified model, yet static merging consistently suffers from parameter interference. While dynamic merging models aim to bridge this gap, many works rely on the costly storage and loading of redundant expert components at inference. In this work, from the perspective of task expert, we view parameter interference as parameter perturbation introduced to each expert during merging process. We show that such parameter perturbations can be modeled as affine transformation, which can be approximated as additive offsets. Motivated by these, we propose Recover Task eXpert (ReTeX), a framework that predicts those offsets, in order to undo parameter interference and recover task-expert performance from a single merged checkpoint. To recover the appropriate expert when task identity is unknown, we introduce a router-free task identifier based on SVD subspace signatures computed offline before inference. At inference, the identifier selects the task whose subspace yields the smallest projection residual for a given input. As a result, ReTeX recovers over 95% of individual-expert performance in both vision and NLP domains, while significantly improving generalization to unseen tasks. Crucially, we also show that the parameter offset prediction leads to emergent adaptive interpolation of expert knowledge for out-of-distribution (OOD) tasks. ReTeX adaptively interpolates seen expert knowledge to handle unseen tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/BAIKLAB/ReTeX

</details>


### [151] [Diagnosing Task Insensitivity in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26918)
*Jingyu Liu,Xiaopeng Wu,Kehan Chen,Chuan Yu,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在长周期任务中的OOD泛化失败源于"任务不敏感性"——模型忽视任务指令而依赖训练时的捷径模式。作者提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization（一种轻量对比正则化器）来增强模型对任务指令的关注，实验证明该方法提升了任务敏感性和OOD泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长周期任务中OOD泛化能力弱，作者识别出关键原因在于任务不敏感性：面对相似但不同的任务时，模型套用训练时的模式，未能真正解决当前任务。

Method: 提出Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization，一种轻量化的对比正则化方法，显式鼓励模型的动作依赖于任务指令，而非忽略指令走捷径。

Result: 该干预方法提升了任务敏感性和OOD泛化能力，同时保持模型对任务token更稳定的注意力分布。

Conclusion: 模型在长周期任务中失败的原因是注意力从任务token漂移到局部观测的优化偏差，所提正则化方法能有效缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Large language models can serve as capable long-horizon agents, but their out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains weak. We identify a key source of this failure as task insensitivity: when faced with similar but distinct tasks, models might apply patterns learned during training and fail to solve the task at hand. We show that models often continue with actions aligned with the original task even when the instruction is semantically corrupted and cannot be directly answered. We further find that, when we replace the task description in a trained prompt with another similar but distinct task, the model may still output the same action. This behavior is accompanied by a consistent training-time attention drift away from task tokens and toward local observations, suggesting an optimization bias toward shortcuts. To mitigate this problem, we propose Task-Perturbed NLL Optimization, a lightweight contrastive regularizer that explicitly encourages action dependence on the task instruction. Extensive evaluations show that our intervention improves task sensitivity and OOD generalization while preserving more stable attention to task tokens.

</details>


### [152] [Where Do CoT Training Gains Land in LLM based Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26935)
*Jingyu Liu,Zhiwen Wang,Yuxin Jing,Huanyu Zhou,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了思维链（CoT）训练究竟提升了什么：是让模型更好地通过推理改变动作，还是让模型更擅长直接从提示中预测动作。实验发现，CoT训练主要提升了直接预测动作（prompt actions）的质量，而非扩大CoT推理带来的优势。基于此，作者提出选择性掩码动作标记监督的方法，提升了模型的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CoT训练的本质提升机制：模型是通过生成推理链改变了动作决策，还是仅仅提升了从提示中直接预测动作的能力？旨在厘清CoT训炼的真实作用，并据此改进训练方法。

Method: 对比两个动作预测方式：prompt actions（无CoT直接预测动作）和CoT actions（先进行CoT推理再预测动作）。跟踪不同检查点下两类动作的质量变化，并比较它们在交互环境中的相对优势。进一步通过选择性掩码部分训练样本中的动作标记监督来验证发现。

Result: 1）prompt action质量随训练显著提升；2）CoT action相对于prompt action的优势在训练过程中保持稳定，说明CoT训练并未扩大CoT推理的优势；3）后期检查点更少根据CoT修订动作，更依赖提示本身；4）选择性掩码动作标记监督能提升跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoT训练的主要贡献在于提升了模型直接从提示中预测动作的能力，而非强化了推理链对动作决策的影响。基于此发现提出的选择性掩码策略有助于提升模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely used in language-model agents, but prior work has shown that verbalized CoT is not always faithful and may instead reflect post-hoc reasoning, which means the model already knows the answer before reasoning. We therefore ask what CoT training is actually improving: is the model getting better at changing its action through generated reasoning, or is it getting better at predicting the action directly from the prompt? We study this question by comparing \emph{prompt actions} (predicting action without CoT) with CoT actions (predicting action with CoT). Across checkpoints, prompt-action quality improves substantially. While interacting with the environment, the relative advantage of CoT actions over prompt actions remains similar, showing that CoT training does not widen the advantage of CoT reasoning, and it helps to improve the quality of prompt actions. We further find that later checkpoints are less likely to revise the action in response to CoT, suggesting greater reliance on the prompt. Motivated by these patterns, we selectively mask action-token supervision on a fraction of training examples. This intervention improves out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [153] [Look-Before-Move: Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention in Dynamic 3D Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26964)
*Jiaming Bian,Bingliang Li,Yuehao Wu,Pichao Wang,Zhi Wang,Hailan Ma,Huadong Mo,Zhenhong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Look-Before-Move框架，通过语义观测契约、蒙特卡洛视点搜索和语义轨迹接地，在动态3D故事世界中实现叙事引导的视觉注意力规划，先决定观察什么再生成平滑运动。在包含50个故事、457个场景的基准上，该方法在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身AI和世界模型在动态3D环境中被动解释观测，缺乏主动决定观察目标的能力；需要一种能先决定观察内容再执行运动的视觉注意力机制。

Method: 提出Look-Before-Move框架：(1) 语义观测契约将导演意图转化为可执行视觉约束；(2) 蒙特卡洛视点搜索寻找符合叙事且几何可行的视点；(3) 语义轨迹接地将选定视点连接为连续、碰撞感知、时间连贯的相机运动。

Result: 构建基于StoryBlender的动态3D故事世界基准（50个故事、457个场景、1585个镜头），实验表明该方法在主体感知、意图一致性和轨迹质量上优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: 在生成相机运动之前先组织视觉注意力对提升叙事引导的3D视觉感知质量至关重要。

Abstract: As embodied AI and world models increasingly operate in dynamic 3D environments, visual perception must move beyond passively interpreting given observations toward actively deciding what to observe. We study this problem through camera planning in dynamic 3D story worlds, where the camera must not only generate smooth motion, but also decide what visual evidence should be acquired before it moves. We formulate this capability as Narrative-Grounded World Visual Attention, where the camera acts as an embodied observer that determines what to observe, how to compose the observation, and how to shift attention over time under narrative intent and physical 3D constraints. To realize this capability, we propose Look-Before-Move, a camera planning framework that separates observation specification from motion execution. It first builds a Semantic Observation Contract to convert directorial intent into executable visual constraints, then performs Monte Carlo Viewpoint Search to find narrative-compliant and geometrically feasible viewpoints, and finally applies Semantic Trajectory Grounding to connect selected viewpoints into continuous, collision-aware, and temporally coherent camera motion. We further construct a dynamic 3D Story World Benchmark based on StoryBlender, covering 50 stories, 457 scenes, and 1585 shots with animated characters, semantic scene configurations, and executable 3D environments. Experiments show that our framework improves subject perception, intent consistency, and trajectory quality over representative baselines, demonstrating the importance of organizing visual attention before generating camera motion.

</details>


### [154] [Einstein World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26969)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Ali Mekky,Zayd M. Kawakibi Zuhri,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Einstein World Models (EWMs)，一种让LLM通过调用世界模块生成视觉-时间推演（如"思想实验"）来辅助推理的框架，超越纯语言推理的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM能否通过可视化反事实事件（而非仅依赖语言）来增强复杂推理能力，弥补纯语言模型在处理超经验现象时的不足。

Method: EWMs将视觉-时间推演（world-module生成的短视频滚动）嵌入LLM推理轨迹中，作为可检验的假设而非最终答案，扩展LLM的工具调用能力至视觉思想实验领域。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，但提出了EWMs作为一种可扩展LLM推理能力的架构蓝图。

Conclusion: 视觉化推演可作为语言推理的互补机制，EWMs为LLM处理需要"想象"或"思想实验"的复杂推理任务提供了新范式。

Abstract: Does intelligence require the ability to reason about phenomena beyond direct experience? It is natural to suspect that some complex thought cannot be captured through language alone. However, of particular concern to this work, is whether visualising counterfactual events can complement language as a mechanism for complex thought. We ask whether LLMs can be trained to utilise such visualisation mechanisms, in a way that benefits their reasoning abilities. Motivated by this question, we propose Einstein World Models. EWMs are a blueprint for LLM-based reasoning systems that place visual-temporal rollouts inside the reasoning trace, allowing them to reason in ways that text alone may not support well. In an EWM, the LLM calls a world-module (not to be confused with a world model), to produce short rollouts of scenes under consideration. The returned rollout is treated not as the answer, but as an inspectable hypothesis that can support later reasoning. Einstein World Models extend the capability of LLMs for tool calling (such as web search or code execution), into the domain of visual thought experiments.

</details>


### [155] [Adaptive Utility driven Resource Orchestration for Resilient AI (AURORA-AI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27005)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Ilias Cherkaoui,Lizy Abraham,Indrakshi Dey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AURORA-AI框架，通过HJB反馈控制、李雅普诺夫稳定性监控和公平感知复合效用的统一闭环策略，在非平稳条件下动态分配计算资源，实现AI系统的弹性、公平性和可解释性，实验证明其性能显著优于五种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统在非平稳的计算、人口统计和操作条件下部署，静态资源分配策略会同时损害预测性能和以人为中心的性质（如公平性和可解释性），亟需一种自适应资源分配框架。

Method: 提出AURORA-AI框架，统一了Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman反馈控制、李雅普诺夫稳定性监控和公平感知复合效用函数，形成闭环策略，在异构AI模型群体中持续重新分配计算预算，以最大化覆盖预测性能、人口统计公平性、成本、延迟、鲁棒性和可解释性的全局效用。

Result: 在压力丰富的离散时间仿真中（注入人口统计偏差冲击、渐进概念漂移和黑天鹅突发事件），AURORA-AI实现黑天鹅事件后即时恢复（基线Static需88步、PPO需22步）；将alpha分位数和超分位数分别提升29%和25%；同时降低人口统计奇偶差距的均值和最大值；增加李雅普诺夫稳定操作步骤的比例。

Conclusion: 基于公平感知的自适应编排与稳定性理论的结合，是实现弹性、以人为中心的AI部署的一条实用且理论上有依据的路径。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are increasingly deployed under non-stationary computational, demographic, and operational conditions in which static resource allocation strategies degrade both predictive performance and human-centric properties such as fairness and explainability. This paper presents AURORA-AI, an Adaptive Utility-driven Resource Orchestration framework for Resilient AI that unifies Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman feedback control, Lyapunov-based stability monitoring, and a fairness-aware composite utility into a single closed-loop policy.The framework continuously redistributes computational budget across a population of heterogeneous AI models so that the global utility, defined jointly over predictive performance, demographic parity, cost, latency, robustness, and interpretability, remains maximised under disruption. The framework is evaluated in a stress-rich discrete-time simulation that concurrently injects demographic bias shocks, gradual concept drift, and abrupt black-swan disruptions, and is compared against five established controllers including Static, Round Robin, Greedy, LinUCB, and a deep reinforcement-learning agent based on Proximal Policy Optimisation. AURORA-AI achieves immediate recovery from the black-swan event compared to eighty-eight time steps for the Static baseline and twenty-two for Proximal Policy Optimisation, lifts the alpha-quantile and the super-quantile by twenty-nine and twenty-five percent respectively, simultaneously reduces the mean and maximum demographic parity gap, and increases the fraction of Lyapunov-stable operating steps. These results indicate that fairness-aware adaptive orchestration grounded in stability theory is a practical and theoretically motivated path toward resilient human-centric AI deployment.

</details>


### [156] [Semantic Early-Stopping for Iterative LLM Agent Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27009)
*Sahil Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体LLM循环的语义早停机制：当连续草稿嵌入的余弦距离不再变化且回答质量停止提升时终止循环。理论证明终止确定性，提出高效的评估协议，实验显示无审判的语义停止器比固定迭代次数节省38%运营token且质量不变，而全质量门控版本因审判开销适得其反。Oracle分析将问题从"何时停止"重新定义为"选择最佳轮次"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定迭代上限（max_iterations）作为循环终止条件是语法层面的"开关"，无法感知答案是否仍在改进，导致简单输入浪费token、困难输入过早截断。需要语义层面的早停机制来解决这一效率与质量的不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 理论层面：证明确定性终止和良定义性，通过机器验证，将距离序列收敛性作为经验性猜想而非过度声称的Banach压缩；2) 评估协议：一次性生成完整轨迹，重放不同停止策略，缓存LLM审判调用，实现严格配对的效率-质量比较；3) 实证研究：在HotpotQA多跳检索问答上测试无审判语义停止器和质量门控变体。

Result: 在60题测试集上，无审判语义停止器相比固定迭代次数节省38%运营token且质量相当（Delta-IS=-0.004, p=0.81）；全质量门控版本因每轮审判成本过高而效果适得其反；Oracle选择最佳轮次比所有实用策略高出+0.115信息分数（p~4e-11）。

Conclusion: 语义早停在多智能体LLM循环中是可行且高效的替代方案；问题的关键从"何时停止"（已解决）转向"哪一轮最佳"（待解决）。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) loops, for example a Writer that drafts and a Critic that revises, are almost always terminated by a fixed iteration cap (max_iterations). This is a syntactic kill-switch: it is blind to whether the answer is still improving, so it over-spends tokens on easy inputs and truncates hard ones. We study semantic early-stopping: the loop halts when consecutive draft embeddings stop changing in meaning (cosine distance with a patience window) and the answer's measured quality stops improving. Our work makes three contributions. First, an honest theoretical footing: we prove deterministic termination and well-definedness and machine-check these claims, while treating the convergence of the distance sequence as an empirically tested conjecture rather than a (previously over-claimed) Banach contraction. Second, a judge-efficient evaluation protocol: we generate each question's full trajectory once, replay every stopping policy over the identical drafts, and cache every LLM-judge call, yielding a strictly paired efficiency-versus-quality comparison at low cost; we further separate operational tokens (charged to a policy) from evaluation tokens (a measurement instrument). Third, an empirical study on multi-hop retrieval-augmented question answering (HotpotQA). On the 60-question test split, a judge-free semantic stopper reduces operational tokens by 38% relative to max_iterations at parity quality (Delta-IS = -0.004, p = 0.81), whereas the full quality-gated variant is counter-productive because its per-round judging dominates cost. An oracle that selects the best round attains +0.115 Information Score over every practical policy (p ~ 4e-11), reframing the problem from "when to stop" (easy) to "which round is best" (open).

</details>


### [157] [How to evaluate clustering with ground truth?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27061)
*Pasi Fränti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于集合匹配的外部聚类有效性指标，推荐使用质心指数（CI）作为直观的聚类级度量，并针对不同需求提供了相应建议：精细化的点级度量推荐配对集指数（PSI），要求各点权重相等时可采用聚类准确度（ACC）或其他集合匹配度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有真实标签的情况下，如何选择合适的聚类外部评价指标是一个重要问题。作者回顾了最常见的基于集合匹配的外部效度指标，旨在为不同应用场景提供指标选择指导。

Method: 综述了多种基于集合匹配的外部聚类有效性指标，包括质心指数（CI）、配对集指数（PSI）和聚类准确度（ACC）等，并从不同维度（聚类级 vs. 点级、是否受簇大小影响等）进行比较分析。

Result: CI作为聚类级度量直观且可解释；PSI提供标准化分数且不受簇大小偏差影响；ACC或集合匹配度量适用于所有点权重相等的情境。

Conclusion: 没有一种指标适用于所有场景，应根据需求选择合适的指标：需要直观可解释的聚类级度量选CI，需要精细点级且去偏的度量选PSI，需要等权重点级度量选ACC。

Abstract: External indexes can be used for cluster evaluation when ground truth is available. We review the most common external validity indexes focusing on set-matching-based measures. We recommend centroid index (CI), because it is an intuitive cluster-level measure with an explainable result. If we need a more fine-tuned, point-level measure, there are more choices. Pair-set index (PSI) provides a normalized score which is not biased by cluster sizes. If all points should matter equally, then clustering accuracy (ACC) or any other set-matching measure is suitable.

</details>


### [158] [Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27136)
*Shicheng Ye,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JERP提出了一种联合学习方法，将LLM智能体的交互经验同时用于更新外部经验规则池和内部模型策略，在AlfWorld和WebShop任务上取得了一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将交互经验分为两种用途：作为自然语言规则在推理时提示（容易解释但会与策略脱节），或用于更新模型参数（改善策略但无法对局部错误进行精确修正）。作者希望结合两者优势，解决稀疏奖励环境下局部错误修正不充分的问题。

Method: JERP维护一个长期经验规则池，在决策时检索任务相关规则并与交互历史一起作为智能体的条件输入。每个回合结束后，使用收集到的轨迹同时优化策略（更新模型参数）和修订规则池（将当前轨迹与参考成功轨迹对比），使规则池与演化中的策略保持对齐。

Result: 在AlfWorld和WebShop两个复杂交互任务上，JERP相比基线方法取得了一致的决策性能提升。

Conclusion: 将外部规则与内部策略联合学习的方式，能够使规则池与演化策略保持同步，同时将稳定有效的行为逐渐吸收到模型内部，有效提升了LLM智能体在多步交互环境中的决策表现。

Abstract: For LLM agents in multi-step interactive environments, a key challenge is to make effective use of accumulated interaction experience. Existing work has typically separated two uses of such experience: keeping it outside the model as natural-language rules for later prompting, or using trajectories and feedback to update the model parameters. The former is easy to interpret but can fall out of sync with the evolving policy; the latter improves the policy more broadly but provides only limited correction for local mistakes in sparse-reward settings. We present Joint Learning of Experiential Rules and Policies for LLM Agents (JERP), which updates a long-term experiential-rule pool and the policy from the same interaction trajectories. At decision time, JERP retrieves task-relevant rules and conditions the agent on them together with the interaction history. After each episode, it uses the collected trajectories both to optimize the policy and to revise the rule pool by comparing current rollouts with reference successful trajectories. This coupling keeps the rule pool aligned with the evolving policy while allowing stable and effective behaviors to be gradually absorbed into the model itself. Experiments on AlfWorld and WebShop show that JERP yields consistent gains in decision performance for complex interactive tasks.

</details>


### [159] [OpenRCA 2.0: From Outcome Labels to Causal Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27154)
*Aoyang Fang,Yifan Yang,Jin'ao Shang,Qisheng Lu,Junjielung Xu,Rui Wang,Songhan Zhang,Yuzhong Zhang,Boxi Yu,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有RCA数据集仅标注根因而缺失因果传播路径，导致LLM任务被简化为模式匹配。本文提出PAVE协议（前向验证机制）构建带步骤级因果标注的OpenRCA 2.0基准（500实例），发现前沿LLM平均仅20.7%能准确恢复根因集，且存在"无根诊断"问题：76.0%能找出正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将其与症状通过已验证的因果路径关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析数据集仅标注根因标签，缺乏从症状到根因的完整因果传播路径，这使得LLM可以通过简单的模式匹配完成RCA任务，无法真正评估其长上下文理解、多步推理和工具使用等智能体能力。

Method: 提出PAVE（逐步标注协议），利用故障注入中的已知干预信息，通过"前向验证"机制（从原因推理到效果，而非从症状向后推断）重建因果传播路径，构建OpenRCA 2.0基准。

Result: 11个前沿LLM平均仅20.7%能完全恢复正确的根因集合；76.0%能找到至少一个正确根因服务，但仅61.5%能将该服务通过已验证的因果路径与观察到的症状关联。

Conclusion: 仅基于结果的评估隐藏了LLM在RCA中的根本缺陷——无法将根因正确地定位在因果传播路径中。步骤级因果标注是可信赖的LLM基的RCA智能体的关键缺失要素。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) poses a holistic test of LLM agentic capabilities, such as long-context understanding, multi-step reasoning, and tool use. However, existing datasets suffer from a fundamental gap: they label only the root cause, not the propagation path connecting it to the observed symptom, which largely simplifies the task to naive pattern matching. To support rigorous evaluation, we introduce PAVE, a step-wise labeling protocol that leverages known interventions from fault injection to reconstruct causal propagation paths. The mechanism is forward verification: reasoning from cause to effect rather than inferring backward from symptoms. Applying PAVE yields OpenRCA 2.0 (500 instances), the first cross-system RCA benchmark with step-wise causal annotations for LLM agents. Across 11 frontier LLMs, recovering the exact root-cause set succeeds in only 20.7% of cases on average. To locate where this difficulty lies, we relax the criterion and find what we call the ungrounded diagnosis: agents identify at least one correct root-cause service in 76.0% of cases, but ground that service in a verified causal propagation path to the observed symptom in only 61.5%. Outcome-only evaluation hides this failure mode; step-wise causal ground truth is the missing piece for trustworthy LLM-based RCA agents.

</details>


### [160] [TOPS: First-Principles Visual Token Pruning via Constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets for Efficient MLLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27161)
*Tinghao Wang,Yichen Guo,Rui Huang,Zheng Lu,Qizhe Zhang,Chenxi Li,Yuan Zhang,Jiajun Cao,Zhirong Shen,Yaosong Du,Guangyan Gan,Wenya Wang,Lin William Cong,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，通过信息论分析，提出三种视觉Token剪枝的基本原则（任务相关性、信息覆盖、语义多样性），并由此设计出无需训练、模型无关的TOPS模块，在多种MLLM上实现高效剪枝，最高去除77.8%的视觉Token且性能不降反升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）虽推理能力强，但视觉Token过多导致计算开销巨大。现有剪枝方法存在注意力标准易保留冗余Token、多样性标准忽略用户指令等问题，缺乏对Token剪枝内在目标的系统性理论表述。

Method: 从第一性原理出发，通过自上而下的信息论分析，提炼出Token最优保留集构建的三个基本原则：任务相关性（Task Relevance）、信息覆盖（Information Coverage）、语义多样性（Semantic Diversity）。基于这三项原则设计TOPS模块，无需训练且模型无关，可直接应用于各种MLLM。

Result: 在7种MLLM骨干网络和14个基准上，TOPS在多种剪枝设置下均优于现有方法。在LLaVA-NeXT上，TOPS剪掉77.8%的视觉Token，7B和13B模型分别保持100.0%和100.6%的性能，表明剪枝冗余Token甚至能缓解幻觉。

Conclusion: 视觉Token剪枝的冗余去除不仅不会损害性能，反而可能缓解多模态模型的幻觉现象，为未来轻量化MLLM设计提供了新思路。TOPS所提出的三项基本原则为Token剪枝提供了系统性的理论指导。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, but their efficiency is limited by the large number of visual tokens, which introduces substantial computational overhead. Visual token pruning offers a natural solution, yet existing methods are imperfect: attention-based criteria tend to retain redundant tokens, while diversity-based criteria are often agnostic to user instructions. Even methods that combine multiple criteria still lack a principled formulation of the intrinsic objective of token pruning. In this paper, we revisit visual token pruning from a first-principles perspective and formulate it as constructing Token Optimal Preservation Sets. Through a top-down information-theoretic analysis, we identify three fundamental principles for effective token selection: Task Relevance, Information Coverage, and Semantic Diversity. Based on these principles, we propose TOPS, a training-free and model-agnostic pruning module that can be applied to various MLLMs. Extensive experiments on 7 MLLM backbones and 14 benchmarks demonstrate that TOPS outperforms prior methods under diverse pruning settings. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, TOPS removes 77.8% of visual tokens while preserving 100.0% and 100.6% performance on its 7B and 13B models, respectively, suggesting that pruning redundant visual tokens can sometimes mitigate hallucination and inspire future lightweight MLLM design.

</details>


### [161] [A Process Harness for Uplifting Legacy Workflows to Agentic BPM: Design and Realization in CUGA FLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27188)
*Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"过程缰绳"机制，通过在确定性工作流引擎外包裹策略驱动的智能体层，实现遗留工作流向Agentic BPM的升级，无需替换底层引擎。基于TDF模型定义了三种策略约束的智能体（TaskAgent、DecisionAgent、FlowAgent），并通过CUGA FLO系统在贷款审批流程中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留工作流系统缺乏智能推理、适应性和监督能力，但完全替换底层引擎成本高、风险大。作者希望在保留现有工作流引擎结构权威的前提下，引入LLM驱动的智能体能力，实现"命令性需求"（确定性执行）与"规范性需求"（智能自治）的统一。

Method: 提出过程缰绳机制：在工作流引擎外围部署策略治理的智能体层，拦截指定控制点注入推理与适应能力。设计TDF（任务-决策-流）模型，包含数据模式和执行语义，将LLM推理分解为三种策略约束的智能体：TaskAgent（知识密集型任务）、DecisionAgent（网关路由）、FlowAgent（运行时流适配）。所有智能体受FRAME策略集约束。实现CUGA FLO系统。

Result: 通过贷款审批工作流案例验证了三种智能体类型及钩子驱动的监管覆盖机制，证明过程缰绳能有效协调确定性执行与智能体自治。

Conclusion: 过程缰绳成功将命令性需求（确定性工作流的结构合规）与规范性需求（策略框架下的智能体自主性）统一，为遗留工作流系统向Agentic BPM演进提供了无需替换引擎的可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce the process harness, a new mechanism for uplifting legacy workflows into Agentic Business Process Management (Agentic BPM) without replacing the underlying workflow engine. A process harness places a policy-governed agentic layer around a deterministic workflow engine, intercepting designated control points to contribute reasoning, adaptation, and oversight while the engine retains structural authority over the process. To define the process harness rigorously, we develop the Task-Decision-Flow (TDF) model, specifying both its data schema and its execution semantics. TDF decomposes LLM reasoning across three policy-governed agent types: a TaskAgent for knowledge-intensive task execution, a DecisionAgent for per-case gateway routing, and a FlowAgent that governs runtime flow adaptation through a principled hook mechanism. Each agent reasons within an explicit policy drawn from the process FRAME, the aggregate policy set governing all LLM calls in the system. We then present CUGA FLO as the design and implementation realization of the TDF model, and demonstrate it on a loan approval workflow that exercises all three agent types and hook-driven regulatory override. The process harness uniquely reconciles imperative requirements, realized through deterministic workflow execution that enforces structural compliance, with normative requirements, realized through policy-framed agentic autonomy invoked at designated control points wherever the process demands it.

</details>


### [162] [Vulnerability of Natural Language Classifiers to Evolutionary Generated Adversarial Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27215)
*Manjinder Singh,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Mohamed Elawady*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GAversary的混合遗传算法，用于对自然语言模型生成对抗性攻击。该方法将目标模型视为黑盒，仅利用模型输出的logit值引导搜索，通过GloVe嵌入改进词替换（变异算子）以提高对抗样本的语义相似性。实验表明，GAversary能显著降低目标模型准确率（最佳情况从76.8%降至5.8%），但扰动词数约为对比方法的两倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间增加约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在自然语言处理中容易受到对抗性攻击，现有攻击方法越来越精确，但往往需要一定程度的模型内部结构访问权限。本文旨在提出一种仅需黑盒访问（logit值）即可生成高质量对抗样本的方法。

Method: 提出GAversary混合遗传算法，将目标模型视为黑盒，仅利用模型输出的logit值引导搜索。该方法的核心创新在于使用GloVe词嵌入来提出词替换（作为遗传算法的变异算子），以提高对抗样本的语义相似性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和知名目标模型上，GAversary相比BAE和A2T攻击方法能更大幅度地降低目标模型准确率（最佳情况从76.8%降至5.8%，而BAE仅降至27.6%）。代价是扰动词数约为对比方法的两倍，语义相似度略低，运行时间增加约5%。

Conclusion: GAversary作为一种黑盒对抗攻击方法，在攻击效果上显著优于BAE和A2T，虽然存在扰动词数较多、语义相似度略低和运行时间略有增加等权衡，但整体上为NLP模型的对抗攻击提供了一种有效的新思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models have achieved impressive performance across various fields but remain vulnerable to adversarial inputs, particularly in NLP, where such attacks can have significant real-world consequences. Adversarial attacks often involve small, semantically similar token replacements to fool NLP models, and recent methods have become more precise by targeting specific vulnerable words, often by exploiting some level of access to the model's internal structure. This paper proposes GAversary, a hybrid Genetic Algorithm (GA) to generate adversarial attacks on natural language models. The GA is able to treat the target model as a black box, requiring only the logit value output by the model to guide the search. GAversary differs from GAs previously proposed for this problem by using GloVe embeddings to propose word replacements (the mutation operator) to improve the semantic similarity of the adversarial examples. GAversary is applied to several benchmark data sets and well-known target models. GAversary is able to substantially reduce the target model's accuracy on test data compared to the BAE and A2T attacks compared against (in the best case, reducing a 76.8% accuracy to 5.8%, compared to BAE's 27.6%). The trade-off is that GAversary perturbs just under twice as many words as the other two methods, with a slightly lower semantic similarity to the original text and around a 5% increase in run-time.

</details>


### [163] [Ask, Don't Judge: Binary Questions for Interpretable LLM Evaluation and Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27226)
*Sangwoo Cho,Kushal Chawla,Pengshan Cai,Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BINEVAL通过将评估标准分解为原子化二元问题，由LLM独立回答，生成可解释的多维评分。该方法在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS上匹配或超越UniEval、G-Eval等强基线，尤其在事实一致性基准上表现突出，并支持迭代提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估存在三大瓶颈：人工评估昂贵且缓慢，词汇指标与开放生成的人类判断相关性差，整体式LLM评判器产生不透明分数难以调试。

Method: 将评估标准分解为原子化二元问题（元提示自动生成），LLM独立回答每个问题，聚合为可解释的多维分数，提供透明度高的逐问题反馈。

Result: 在SummEval、Topical-Chat和QAGS上匹配或超越UniEval、G-Eval等基线；在QAGS等事实一致性基准上表现尤其强劲；更好匹配人类评分分布，避免天花板效应；问题级反馈支持迭代提示优化。

Conclusion: BINEVAL是一个任务无关、无需训练、可解释的评估框架，兼具强实证性能与实用诊断和优化价值。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM outputs remains a major bottleneck in NLP: human evaluation is expensive and slow, lexical metrics correlate poorly with human judgments on open-ended generation, and holistic LLM judges often produce opaque scores that are hard to debug. We propose BINEVAL, a framework that decomposes evaluation criteria into atomic binary questions and aggregates the resulting verdicts into interpretable, multi-dimensional scores. Given a task prompt, a meta-prompt generates fine-grained evaluation questions, and an LLM answers them independently for each output, yielding transparent question-level feedback together with calibrated overall scores. This decomposition makes evaluation easier to inspect, easier to diagnose, and directly usable for prompt improvement. Across SummEval, Topical-Chat, and QAGS, BINEVAL matches or outperforms strong baselines including UniEval and G-Eval, with especially strong results on factual consistency benchmarks such as QAGS. Beyond competitive correlation with human judgments, BINEVAL better matches human score distributions and avoids the ceiling effects common in prior LLM judges, leading to better discrimination between borderline and clearly flawed outputs. We further show that the same question-level feedback supports iterative prompt optimization, improving evaluator prompts on summarization and generation prompts on IFBench under both self-update and cross-model update settings. Overall, BINEVAL provides a task-agnostic, training-free, and interpretable evaluation framework that combines strong empirical performance with practical diagnostic and optimization value.

</details>


### [164] [EO-WM: A Physically Informed World Model for Probabilistic Earth Observation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27277)
*Junwei Luo,Shuai Yuan,Zhenya Yang,Yansheng Li,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EO-WM，一种用于多光谱地球观测预测的视频扩散Transformer模型。该模型引入物理信息条件框架，区分气候基线与天气异常，并累积异常强迫信号以捕捉持续热害和干旱胁迫。同时提出两个诊断基准（极端夏季基准和季节性匹配对基准）来评估天气响应行为。实验表明，EO-WM在NDVI下降幅度误差上相对降低5.63%，方向命中率提升7.80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能充分捕捉EO预测中部分观测、天气驱动世界建模的特性：确定性模型将不确定性坍缩为单一预测；扩散模型将天气变量视为无差别条件信号；现有基准主要关注重建精度，而非预测是否对天气强迫变化做出正确响应。

Method: 提出EO-WM，基于视频扩散Transformer架构。核心创新是物理信息条件框架：(1)通过不同条件路径分离气候基线与天气异常；(2)随时间累积异常强迫信号以捕捉持续热害和干旱胁迫。同时设计极端夏季基准（严重感知的植被退化预测）和季节性匹配对基准（评估天气强迫变化下的响应保真度）。

Result: NDVI下降幅度预测误差相对降低5.63%，方向命中率相对提升7.80%，同时在标准像素级指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: EO-WM通过物理信息条件框架有效提升了对极端天气事件的预测能力，为EO预测任务提供了更符合物理规律的条件建模方式，代码和基准将开源。

Abstract: Earth Observation (EO) forecasting aims to predict future Earth surface dynamics from satellite observations under changing meteorological conditions. In this paper, we view this task as a partially observed, weather-driven world modeling problem, in which weather acts as a conditioning signal, while forecasting remains uncertain due to sparse observations and unobserved land-surface states. However, existing methods do not fully capture this setting: deterministic models collapse uncertainty into a single future prediction, while diffusion-based methods typically treat weather variables as undifferentiated conditioning signals, and existing benchmarks focus mainly on reconstruction accuracy rather than whether forecasts respond correctly to changed weather forcing.We introduce EO-WM, a video diffusion transformer for multispectral EO forecasting. EO-WM incorporates a physically informed conditioning framework that represents meteorological forcing through a climatological baseline, weather anomalies, and cumulative physical stress signals. Specifically, it separates baseline and anomaly through distinct conditioning pathways, and accumulates anomalous forcing over time to capture sustained heat and drought stress. To evaluate weather-response behavior beyond standard metrics, we introduce two diagnostic benchmarks: an Extreme Summer Benchmark for severity-aware prediction of vegetation degradation under extreme weather, and a Seasonal Matched-Pair Benchmark for testing response fidelity under changed weather forcing. Experiments show that EO-WM reduces the error in predicted Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI) decline amplitude by a relative 5.63% and improves directional hit rate by a relative 7.80%, while remaining competitive on standard pixel-level metrics. The benchmarks and model will be made open-source at https://github.com/Luo-Z13/EO-WM.

</details>


### [165] [Simulation-based inference for rapid Bayesian parameter estimation in epidemiological models: a comparison with MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27286)
*Alina Bazarova,Johann Fredrik Jadebeck,Henrik Zunker,Carolina J. Klett-Tammen,Torben Heinsohn,Wolfgang Wiechert,Katharina Noeh,Stefan Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模拟的推断（SBI）作为MCMC的替代方法，用于贝叶斯校准机械流行病学模型。结果表明，SBI在保持与MCMC相当的后验分布和预测性能的同时，显著降低了计算时间（从数小时缩短至几分钟），为近实时疫情分析提供了高效框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCMC在校准机械流行病学模型时计算成本高昂，尤其对于高维非线性系统和需要反复近实时分析的情景，亟需一种更高效的贝叶斯推断方法。

Method: 采用基于神经后验估计的模拟推断（SBI），以德国2020年COVID-19 ICU占用数据为基础，对SECIR流行病学模型进行贝叶斯校准，并与MCMC在31天和201天窗口期进行对比，使用Wasserstein距离和KL散度定量评估后验分布一致性。

Result: 31天窗口期：SBI与MCMC后验分布高度一致（约60-70秒 vs 约1000秒）；201天窗口期：SBI保持主要后验结构（约157秒 vs 超19000秒）。

Conclusion: SBI为机械流行病学模型的贝叶斯校准提供了快速且计算高效的框架，支持反复近实时推断和快速疫情分析。

Abstract: Mechanistic epidemiological models are widely used to support infectious disease forecasting and public-health decision making. Bayesian calibration of such models is commonly performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), which can become computationally expensive for high-dimensional nonlinear systems and repeated near-real-time analyses. Here, we investigate simulation-based inference (SBI) using neural posterior estimation as a scalable alternative for Bayesian calibration of a mechanistic SECIR epidemiological model using COVID-19 intensive care unit (ICU) occupancy data from Germany during 2020. We compared SBI and MCMC across multiple epidemic phases using both 31-day inference windows and a substantially more challenging 201-day reconstruction problem involving multiple transmission change points. Posterior agreement was evaluated quantitatively using Wasserstein distances and Kullback-Leibler divergences together with posterior predictive checks. Across the 31-day windows, SBI recovered posterior distributions in strong agreement with MCMC while accurately reproducing observed ICU trajectories. In the 201-day setting, SBI preserved the dominant posterior structure despite increased uncertainty. SBI, by combining CPU and GPU resources, substantially reduced computational runtime compared with MCMC, which was restricted to running on CPUs. Whereas MCMC required approximately 1000 seconds for the 31-day inference problems, SBI achieved comparable posterior and predictive performance in approximately 60-70 seconds on a single GPU. For the 201-day inference problem, SBI required an average of 157 seconds, while the MCMC runs took over 19,000 seconds. Our results demonstrate that SBI provides a rapid and computationally efficient framework for Bayesian calibration of mechanistic epidemiological models, supporting repeated near-real-time inference and rapid outbreak analysis.

</details>


### [166] [Prompt Injection in Automated Résumé Screening with Large Language Models: Single and Multi-Injection Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27287)
*Preet Baxi,Jiannan Xu,Jane Yi Jiang,Stefanus Jasin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提示注入在LLM简历筛选中的影响：当候选人质量相近且少数人注入时效果显著，但普及后效果骤降；质量差异大时效果有限但可能引发公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被广泛用于简历筛选，候选人可能策略性地操纵算法系统，因此需要研究提示注入（prompt injection）对自动化招聘的影响及其边界条件。

Method: 通过控制实验，在简历质量同质/异质、注入者多/少等不同条件下，测试提示注入对LLM排名结果的影响。

Result: 1) 简历质量同质且少数候选人注入时，提示注入可靠提升排名；2) 注入普及后效果迅速衰减；3) 质量异质时平均效果较弱，但偶尔低质量候选人可超越高质量候选人。

Conclusion: LLM筛选系统在操纵罕见且候选人质量差异小时最脆弱；提示注入存在公平性隐患。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to screen and rank job applicants, creating incentives for candidates to strategically manipulate algorithmic hiring systems. We study prompt injection in automated résumé screening, defined as subtle self-promotional text that introduces no new qualifications but is designed to influence LLM evaluations. Using controlled experiments, we show that prompt injection reliably improves applicant rankings when résumé quality is homogeneous and few candidates inject. However, its effectiveness rapidly diminishes as more candidates inject, collapsing when manipulation becomes widespread. When candidate quality is heterogeneous, prompt injection is less effective on average, but can occasionally allow lower-quality candidates to outrank higher-quality ones, raising fairness concerns. Overall, LLM-based screening is most vulnerable when manipulation is rare and candidate quality differences are small. Code and resources are publicly available at: https://github.com/preetb1199/Prompt_Injection_ACL26

</details>


### [167] [When Does Combining Language Models Help? A Co-Failure Ceiling on Routing, Voting, and Mixture-of-Agents Across 67 Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27288)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多模型LLM系统（路由、投票、级联等）的增益存在理论上限，该上限由所有模型同时答错同一查询的概率（beta）决定。平均成对误差相关性（rho）无法识别beta。在67个模型、21个提供商的实验中，beta的实际值远高于高斯copula模型的预测值（2.5倍差距），且高质量异构集成的表现优于同质集成，但组合模型很少能超越单一最佳模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究热衷于构建多模型LLM系统以提高准确性，但忽略了这类系统增益的理论上限。作者希望揭示这个被忽视的上限——所有模型共同出错的概率（beta），并证明常用的误差相关性指标（rho）无法准确衡量这一上限。

Method: 理论推导证明：当策略输出为某一组成模型的答案时，准确率不超过1-beta（beta为所有模型对同一查询都答错的概率）。使用Clopper-Pearson方法对beta进行有限样本置信界估计。基于67个模型、21个提供商的数学和代码任务进行大规模实验，采用四列相关（tetrachoric correlation）校准的单因子模型和高斯copula进行对比分析。

Result: 开放数学任务中，实际beta=0.052，而67模型高斯copula预测仅为0.023（低估约2.5倍，90%置信区间1.7-3.4）。代码任务中beta=0.079。GPQA-Diamond自由回答形式下beta=0.127（五位评审kappa 0.73-0.92）。低rho异质集成优于高rho同质Self-MoA，但组合模型很少超越单一最佳模型。

Conclusion: 多模型系统的增益上限由所有模型共同出错的概率（beta）决定，而非常用相关性指标。增益来源于不同模型在不同问题上失败，而非简单增加模型数量。在没有强查询级路由信号的情况下，组合模型通常不优于单一最佳模型。

Abstract: Multi-model LLM systems such as routing, voting, cascades, fusion, and mixture-of-agents are used to beat single-model accuracy. We show that their gain is capped by a quantity the field rarely reports. For any policy whose output is one member model answer, accuracy cannot exceed one minus beta, where beta is the rate at which every model is wrong on the same query. In contrast, the usual diagnostic, average pairwise error correlation rho, cannot identify beta: error laws with identical marginals and pairwise correlations can have different all-wrong rates. A Clopper-Pearson bound on beta gives a finite-sample certificate on the largest gain any router, vote, or cascade could deliver before training a router.
  Across 67 models from 21 providers, a tetrachoric-calibrated single-factor model still underprices the all-wrong tail: on open-ended mathematics, observed beta is 0.052 versus 0.023 under the full 67-model Gaussian copula, about 2.5 times underpricing, with 90 percent CI 1.7 to 3.4 and k equals 17. The effect recurs on execution-graded code, where beta is 0.079. Re-asking the same GPQA-Diamond questions in free-response rather than multiple-choice form reopens the tail, with beta 0.127 and a five-judge panel with kappa 0.73 to 0.92, locating co-failure in answer format rather than subject. At matched quality, low-rho heterogeneous ensembles beat high-rho Self-MoA, but on checkable tasks in our pool, combining models rarely beats the single best model without a strong query-level routing signal. Gains come from models failing on different questions, not from adding more models.

</details>


### [168] [Language-Based Digital Twins for Elderly Cognitive Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27334)
*Mohammad Mehdi Hosseini,Mohammad H. Mahoor,Hiroko H. Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于语言数字孪生的框架，利用大语言模型结合文体特征和上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为，并通过多条件变分自编码器评估认知一致性，实验表明该方法能有效保留个体特征并在认知评估中优于基线GPT模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期检测轻度认知障碍（MCI）仍然具有挑战性，语言和对话模式可作为非侵入性生物标志物。数字孪生为个性化医疗提供了新范式，但目前缺乏基于语言行为构建认知健康数字孪生的研究。

Method: 1）利用大语言模型结合文体特征和上下文元数据模拟老年人对话行为；2）提出多头条件变分自编码器（cVAE）联合衡量重建质量和预测认知评分。

Result: 在I-CONECT数据集上，数字孪生保留了身份特征，重建质量和MoCA预测误差与真实数据相当，且优于GPT生成的基线响应。

Conclusion: 基于语言的数字孪生可作为可扩展、非侵入性的个性化连续认知健康监测方法。

Abstract: Digital twins have emerged as a promising paradigm for personalized healthcare, enabling modeling of individual behavior and health trajectories. In cognitive health, early detection of Mild Cognitive Impairment (MCI) remains challenging, where language and conversational patterns serve as non-invasive biomarkers. In this work, we propose a language-based digital twin framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) to mimic the conversational behavior of elderly individuals by incorporating stylometric cues and contextual metadata. To evaluate fidelity and cognitive consistency, we introduce a multi-head conditional variational autoencoder (cVAE) that jointly measures reconstruction quality and predicts cognitive scores. Experiments on the I-CONECT dataset show that the digital twin preserves identity-specific characteristics and achieves reconstruction and MoCA prediction errors comparable to real data, while outperforming baseline GPT-generated responses. These results highlight the potential of language-based digital twins as a scalable and non-invasive approach for personalized and continuous cognitive health monitoring.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VMTAD，一种基于视频记忆Transformer的无监督异常检测方法，用于农业机器人在动态场景中的实时障碍物检测，在油菜籽数据集上达到了SOTA性能（检测AUROC 0.973，分割AUROC 0.997），轻量版本推理仅需14ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全传感器（如LiDAR）无法检测植物冠层下方的障碍物；基于监督学习的视觉方法对未见过的障碍物泛化能力差；现有的无监督异常检测方法难以处理机器人移动带来的动态场景变化。

Method: VMTAD采用Transformer架构并配备专用记忆模块，通过处理前序帧的编码表示来利用时间上下文信息，仅使用正常操作的图像进行无监督训练，无需任何数据标签。

Result: 在'Grillion'农业机器人的挑战性油菜籽数据集上，VMTAD达到检测AUROC 0.973、分割AUROC 0.997；轻量变体在保持高精度的同时实现14ms实时推理，满足机器人的安全制动距离要求。

Conclusion: VMTAD通过引入时间记忆机制有效解决了动态农业场景中的无监督异常检测难题，在精度和实时性上均达到了实际部署要求，为农业机器人安全运行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [170] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
*Jannik Drotleff,Samuel Tovey,Paul Hohenberger,Christoph Lohrmann,Julian Hoßbach,Konstantin Nikolaou,Christian Holm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于物理的血毛细血管网络模拟环境，结合真实流体力学、红血球动力学和解剖学分支结构，训练深度强化学习智能体通过趋化性进行导航。智能体发现了多种通用策略类型，并能实现靶向阻塞/解阻塞毛细血管血流，恢复健康水平，证明RL可用于复杂生物环境中的自主微机器人干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微机器人导航的强化学习研究局限于理想化几何环境，忽略了体内复杂流体场、分支结构和细胞障碍，缺乏物理真实感。

Method: 构建物理真实的血毛细血管网络仿真（含流体力学、红血球动力学、解剖分支结构），使用深度强化学习训练智能体通过趋化性导航。

Result: 绘制了机器人尺寸与速度的物理极限图，发现布朗运动与流动主导的"禁止区域"；智能体自主发现多种通用策略（run-and-rotate、search-and-sit等）；无需重新训练即可实现靶向阻塞/解阻塞，恢复血流至健康基线水平。

Conclusion: 强化学习是开发复杂生物环境中自主微机器人干预策略的可行框架。

Abstract: Autonomous microrobots navigating biological vasculature could enable targeted drug delivery and thrombolysis, yet training control policies for realistic environments remains an open challenge. Prior reinforcement learning (RL) studies of microrobotic navigation have been limited to idealized geometries that omit complex hydrodynamic flow fields, confined branching structures, and dense cellular obstacles found in vivo. Here, we develop a physically grounded simulation of a blood capillary network, incorporating realistic hydrodynamic flow fields, explicit red blood cell dynamics, and anatomically derived branching geometry, and train deep RL agents to navigate it via chemotaxis. We systematically map the physical limits of navigation across robot size and swimming speed, revealing a forbidden regime where Brownian motion and flow overcome propulsion. Successful agents independently discover multiple universal strategy types, including run-and-rotate and energy-efficient search-and-sit policies, regardless of robot parameters. Without retraining, these agents perform targeted blocking and unblocking of capillary flow, restoring throughput to healthy baseline levels. These results establish RL as a viable framework for developing autonomous microrobotic intervention strategies in complex biological environments.

</details>


### [171] [RMTL: Reinforced Micro-task Learning for Long-Horizon Manipulation with VLM Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26175)
*Anıl Can Ateş,Orhan Kahraman,Cihan Topal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL)，将机器人操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，每个微任务使用独立的VLM奖励提示，并通过反向课程学习和分层策略实现更高效的学习，相比单提示VLM奖励方法在Fetch环境中表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，单提示VLM奖励在长时序操作任务中奖励信号过于粗糙，近乎平坦，导致智能体难以感知早期进步；而手动设计稠密奖励或从人类偏好学习又成本高昂。

Method: 将操作任务分解为多个语言描述的微任务，每个微任务有独立提示词；使用多视角VLM奖励计算当前微任务的奖励并平均多视角结果减少遮挡影响；采用反向课程学习逐步增加初始条件难度；先使用固定距离规则训练PPO worker，再替换为学习的分层管理器实现完全学习的层次化策略。

Result: 在Fetch操作环境中，RMTL相比单提示VLM奖励方法提供了更信息丰富的奖励信号，实现了更快的学习速度。

Conclusion: 将VLM奖励分解为微任务特定的语言提示可以显著提升语言引导强化学习在机器人操作中的可扩展性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for robotic manipulation often requires manually designing a dense reward function, which is difficult to tune and often fragile, or learning a reward from human demonstrations or preferences, which can be expensive. A recent line of work uses pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as zero-shot reward models, replacing these costs with a single text prompt. However, we argue that a single global prompt is too coarse for long-horizon manipulation tasks with randomized initial conditions. The single-prompt VLM reward is near-flat for much of the trajectory, making early progress hard for the agent to detect. We propose Reinforced Micro-Task Learning (RMTL), an approach that decomposes a manipulation task into a small set of language-described micro-tasks and trains the agent to switch between them. At each step, the agent receives a multi-view VLM reward computed using the prompt of the currently active micro-task and averaged across multiple camera views to reduce the effect of view-specific occlusions. A reverse curriculum gradually exposes the agent to harder initial conditions, while a PPO worker is first trained with a fixed distance-based rule that selects the active micro-task. We then replace this rule with a learned hierarchical manager, turning rule-based phase selection into a fully learned hierarchical policy. We instantiate RMTL on the Fetch manipulation environment using three short stage-specific prompts and without additional prompt tuning. Experiments show that RMTL provides more informative reward signals than single-prompt VLM rewards, enabling faster learning. These results suggest that decomposing VLM rewards into micro-task-specific language prompts can substantially improve the scalability of language-guided reinforcement learning for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [172] [LiMoDE: Rethinking Lifelong Robot Manipulation from a Mixture-of-Dynamic-Experts Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26183)
*Zhihao Gu,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LiMoDE，一种基于动态专家混合架构的两阶段终身机器人操作学习方案。第一阶段通过动态MoE结构学习多任务先验知识，第二阶段通过终身MoE适应机制动态组合专家实现新任务持续适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么通过参数高效微调解决灾难性遗忘但无法提取可复用技能并建模技能间交互，要么通过学习提示来解决但仍有不足。因此需要一种能持续学习、有效提取可复用技能并建模技能交互的机器人操作学习方法。

Method: 提出两阶段学习方案：1）多任务预训练阶段：设计动态MoE结构，根据运动信息激活不同数量的异构专家来应对不同短期操作任务；2）任务适应阶段：设计终身MoE适应机制，学习终身专家并动态将其与冻结的预训练专家组合，实现新任务知识迁移。

Result: 在模拟终身学习基准和真实世界任务上进行了广泛实验，证明了LiMoDE在引入适量额外可训练参数和推理开销的情况下，实现了优越的性能和强大的终身适应能力。

Conclusion: LiMoDE通过动态专家混合架构有效解决了机器人终身操作学习中的灾难性遗忘和技能复用问题，在性能和适应能力上均表现优异，且额外开销可控。

Abstract: Building a generalist robot that can leverage prior knowledge for continuous task adaptation remains a significant challenge. Previous works alleviate the catastrophic forgetting problem by parameter-efficient fine-tuning for single-task adaptation. However, they fail to extract reusable skills and model the interaction with other skills effectively. Recent works try to address these issues by learning prompts. Differently, this paper presents an architectural perspective on the Lifelong Mixture of Dynamic Experts (\textit{LiMoDE}), a novel two-stage learning scheme for lifelong robot manipulation. Specifically, a dynamic MoE structure is first proposed in the multi-task pre-training stage to learn prior knowledge, where a varied number of heterogeneous experts are activated based on the motion information to address different short-term manipulations. Subsequently, in the task adaptation stage, we design a lifelong MoE adaptation mechanism % (LiMoEAM) that learns lifelong experts and dynamically combines them with frozen ones for new tasks, facilitating the knowledge transfer during adaptation. The proposed \textit{LiMoDE} is evaluated on both the simulated lifelong learning benchmark and real-world tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving superior performance and strong lifelong adaptation by introducing a moderate number of additional trainable parameters and inference overhead.

</details>


### [173] [Morphology-Specific Closed-Loop Control of Logarithmic-Spiral Continuum Arms via Online Jacobian Error Compensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26188)
*Partha Datta,Yi Jin,Wei Lin,C. Chase Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次为对数螺旋连续体臂提出了形态特定的闭环任务空间控制框架，结合解析雅可比与在线补偿，在仿真中实现了高精度轨迹跟踪、姿态抑制和多种灵巧操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有对数螺旋机器人具备可扩展制造和灵巧抓取能力，但缺乏逆运动学和闭环控制，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 基于分段肌腱驱动模型和对数螺旋运动学推导解析任务空间雅可比，结合Broyden割线更新与卡尔曼滤波进行在线误差补偿，实现闭环控制。

Result: 在平面和空间仿真中，该方法相较PCC基线持续降低跟踪误差、抑制姿态漂移，并在障碍辅助抓取、全臂自适应抓取、多臂协同操作中表现优异。

Conclusion: 结合对数螺旋形态与在线雅可比补偿可实现对高度欠驱动连续体机械臂的精确、鲁棒、可扩展控制，为硬件实现和软体机器人学习增强控制建立了物理基础。

Abstract: Logarithmic spirals are ubiquitous in biological appendages and provide an attractive morphology for continuum manipulators capable of reaching, wrapping, and grasping. Recently reported logarithmic-spiral robots demonstrated scalable fabrication and versatile grasping but lacked inverse kinematics and closed-loop control. This work presents the first morphology-specific closed-loop task-space control framework for logarithmic-spiral continuum arms. A segmented tendon-driven model with a centerline backbone and equilateral tendon routing is developed in MuJoCo to capture tapered compliance and contact dynamics. An analytical task-space Jacobian is derived directly from the logarithmic-spiral kinematics and combined with online Jacobian error compensation using a Broyden secant update and Kalman-filter estimation. The resulting controller continuously corrects modeling errors arising from nonlinear deformation, contact, and geometric mismatch. The framework is validated through planar and spatial simulations, including trajectory tracking, attitude regulation, disturbance rejection, three-dimensional position tracking, and simultaneous position-orientation control. Compared with a piecewise-constant-curvature (PCC) baseline, the proposed method consistently reduces tracking errors, suppresses attitude drift, and maintains a bounded Jacobian estimation error. The controller is further applied to morphology-enabled manipulation tasks, including obstacle-assisted reach-wrap-release motions, adaptive whole-arm grasping, and cooperative multi-arm object handling. Results demonstrate that combining logarithmic-spiral morphology with online Jacobian compensation enables accurate, robust, and scalable control of highly underactuated continuum manipulators. The proposed framework establishes a physics-grounded baseline for future hardware implementation and learning-augmented soft robotic control.

</details>


### [174] [OmniContact: Chaining Meta-Skills via Contact Flow for Generalizable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26201)
*Runyi Yu,Xiaoyi Lin,Ji Ma,Yinhuai Wang,Koukou Luo,Jiahao Ji,Huayi Wang,Wenjia Wang,Runhan Zhang,Ping Tan,Ting Wu,Ruoli Dai,Qifeng Chen,Lei Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以**接触流**为核心的分层框架，包含低层技能库CF-Track和高层规划器CF-Gen，配合OmniContact数据集，在长时程人形机器人操作任务上显著超越基线方法，并支持与VLM结合实现语义化任务分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在长时程人形机器人操作中存在两难：显式交互表示精度高但难以高层规划，隐式嵌入紧凑但缺乏可解释性。需要一个既能精确执行又能可靠组合的中间表征。

Method: 提出以**接触流**为核心的分层框架：低层策略CF-Track学习统一的操作技能库；高层模块CF-Gen启发式合成未来接触流序列。同时收集OmniContact数据集（基于动捕的人-物交互语料库）。

Result: Carry Box任务98.7%成功率，Push-Stack Boxes任务76.5%成功率，在元技能和技能链式组合上分别平均超越基线40.9%和66.5%。

Conclusion: 接触流是一种高效且可解释的中间表征，能够统一技能学习、失败恢复和长时程组合，并天然支持与VLM结合实现语义化复杂行为。

Abstract: Learning long-horizon humanoid loco-manipulation poses a dual challenge: it requires not only the robust execution of meta-skills but also their seamless, closed-loop chaining equipped with autonomous recovery. Existing approaches remain limited: explicit humanoid-object interaction representations offer precision but are notoriously difficult for high-level planning, whereas implicit skill embeddings are compact but lack the interpretability required for reliable composition. We propose \ours, a hierarchical framework centered on \textbf{contact flow (CF)}, a compact representation consisting of key body trajectories and time-series binary contact signals. Leveraging this shared interface, our low-level policy \textbf{CF-Track} learns a unified library of loco-manipulation skills, while our high-level module \textbf{CF-Gen} heuristically synthesizes future contact-flow sequences. To support this setting, we additionally collect the OmniContact dataset, a MoCap-based HOI corpus for humanoid loco-manipulation (Appendix~\ref{sec:dataset}). Together, they enable robust execution, autonomous failure recovery, and flexible composition of meta-skills for long-horizon tasks. Experiments show that OmniContact achieves \(98.7\%\) success on \textit{Carry Box} and \(76.5\%\) on \textit{Push-Stack Boxes}, outperforming prior baselines by average margins of \(40.9\%\) in meta-skill and \(66.5\%\) in skill chaining. Besides, our framework naturally integrates with VLMs for semantic task decomposition, enabling complex, semantically grounded loco-manipulation behaviors, such as arranging scattered boxes into a heart shape.

</details>


### [175] [RoboTales: ROBOTic Anthropomorphic LEarning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26213)
*Andrew Chen,Ju-Hung Chen,Phurinat Pinyomit,Alexis E. Block*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTales是一个低成本机器人说故事系统，通过表达性袜子木偶动画叙述，在Baxter机器人上自主实现，实验表明木偶模式比仅手势模式获得更高的人机交互评分和更好的故事回忆效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低成本、屏幕自由的机器人木偶叙事方式，替代被动媒体，提升儿童参与感和叙事理解能力。

Method: 在Baxter机器人上实现RoboTales系统，同步叙述、手势和嘴部动作进行木偶表演，并与仅手势模式进行对比实验。

Result: 木偶式讲故事在HRIES评分和故事回忆方面均优于仅手势模式。

Conclusion: 具身木偶表演能增强参与度和叙事理解，系统模块化且平台无关，适合未来部署于儿童学习环境。

Abstract: RoboTales is a low-cost robotic storytelling system that animates narratives using expressive sock puppetry. Implemented autonomously on a Baxter robot as a test case, RoboTales synchronizes narration, gestures, and mouth movements to perform character-driven stories. In a pilot study, puppet-based storytelling outperformed a gesture-only mode, producing higher HRIES ratings and improved story recall, suggesting that embodied puppetry enhances engagement and narrative comprehension. Designed to be modular and platform-agnostic, RoboTales can be adapted to other manipulators and offers a screen-free alternative to passive media, supporting future deployment in child-centered learning environments.

</details>


### [176] [TaskNPoint: How to Teach Your Humanoid to Hit a Backhand in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26215)
*Blake Werner,Ilona Demler,Pietro Perona,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TaskNPoint训练协议，通过人类教练提供少量示范和交互窗口定义，配合单GPU下不足一小时的训练，让G1人形机器人学会网球正反手击球、踢足球和抓取放置等动态技能，并实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为学习动态技能（如打网球）的关键在于掌握轨迹中少量关键交互窗口（如球拍接触球前的20厘米），而非大量重复观看。这启发他们将"教练-学习者"分工范式引入机器人训练，由人类提供示范和窗口定义，机器人自主学习填充完整动作轨迹。

Method: TaskNPoint协议：人类教练提供4类输入（离散技能集、每技能一次演示、交互窗口定义、目标）；训练时随机采样目标位置实现单次演示零样本泛化；在物理仿真环境中学习动作轨迹并增强鲁棒性；无需每个任务单独调奖励函数。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证：成功学会正反手击球（应对人类投球）、踢足球、抓取放置新位置物体；仅需少量人类视频示范；单GPU训练时间不足一小时；无需任务特定奖励调参。

Conclusion: 证明了"教练-学习者"分工范式对机器人动态技能学习有效，少量示范+短时训练即可实现复杂动态技能的泛化能力，为机器人学习提供了一种高效、实用的训练框架。

Abstract: How do we learn to hit a tennis backhand? Not from a thousand hours of tennis tournaments on TV - we work with a coach and practice. We argue this is also the right recipe for teaching dynamic skills to humanoid robots. This follows from a structural property of dynamic skills: the outcome is decided by a short, crucial portion of the trajectory - for a backhand, the ~20cm of racket travel around ball contact. Getting this interaction window right requires coordinating the whole motion, so that control, physics, and morphology act in concert. Learning thus reduces to mastering a handful of distinct actions and, for each, practicing until the window comes out right. To this end, we introduce TaskNPoint, a training protocol which makes the coach-learner division of labor explicit. The human coach contributes four inputs: a discrete set of skills (e.g. different shots), one demonstration per skill, identification of the interaction window, and the goal. Learning in a physically realistic simulation environment fills in each action trajectory and provides robustness to unmodeled events. Crucially, randomized target sampling during training lets a single demonstration generalize zero-shot to unseen goal locations. We test this approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid that hits forehands and backhands against balls thrown by a human, kicks incoming soccer balls, and picks and places boxes from novel locations. We find that learning is successful from short human video demonstrations and under an hour of training on a single GPU, with no per-task reward tuning.

</details>


### [177] [NavIsaacLab: Generating Realistic Crowd via Parallel Robot Learning for Benchmarking Human-aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26265)
*Bingyi Xia,Han Bao,Jingyu Zhu,Hanjing Ye,Yuhan Pang,Guangcheng Chen,Liang Lin,Wenjun Xu,Jiankun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavIsaacLab框架，通过基于物理和照片级真实感的仿真平台解决人类感知导航中场景数据稀缺的问题，支持GPU并行仿真、数据驱动的人体行为建模和多尺度场景基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人类感知导航研究面临高质量多样化场景数据稀缺的瓶颈，现有仿真平台依赖手工规则模拟行人行为、缺乏真实传感器信号（如视觉反馈），难以支撑可靠导航策略的训练与评估。

Method: 基于Isaac Lab平台，集成照片级真实感渲染与GPU并行仿真；采用轨迹扩散模型+对抗运动学习控制器的数据驱动方法模拟逼真的行人运动；构建跨尺度的多样化场景基准。

Result: 实现了实时准确的3D视觉反馈、可控的基于物理的行人仿真，以及面向SOTA导航方法的鲁棒基准测试能力。

Conclusion: NavIsaacLab通过物理真实感仿真弥补了现有平台在场景多样性和传感器保真度上的不足，为安全自然的人机共享空间导航策略训练与评估提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Robot autonomous navigation that accounts for surrounding human activities is crucial for ensuring both safety and natural human-robot interaction in real-world environments shared by humans and robots. Simulation of complex and diverse navigation scenarios serves as the foundation for training reliable robot navigation policies and accurately evaluating the performance of algorithms, offering an efficient alternative to manual supervision of real data. However, current human-aware navigation research faces significant challenges due to the scarcity of diverse, high-quality scene data. Existing simulation platforms often rely on handcrafted rules to approximate pedestrian behavior and lack the capability to provide extensive sensor signals, typically assuming perfect observations. To address these limitations, this paper presents NavIsaacLab, a comprehensive framework for benchmarking and training human-aware navigation policies through physics-based and photo-realistic simulations of pedestrians and scenes. Based on Isaac Lab, the proposed framework employs photo-realistic scene rendering capabilities and supports parallel simulation on GPU, delivering real-time and accurate 3D visual feedback to robots. To enhance the realism of human behavior, a data-driven approach is employed that incorporates a trajectory diffusion model and an adversarial motion learning controller, enabling controllable, physics-based pedestrian simulation. Furthermore, the integration of diverse cross-scale scenes provides a robust benchmark for state-of-the-art human-aware navigation methods.

</details>


### [178] [Racing a Wheeled Quadruped: Active Load Transfer Mitigation via Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26313)
*Marla Eisman,Brian Lam,Samuel Sonnino,Francesco Borrelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合MPC和RL的分层控制框架，用于轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的主动侧倾控制，实现了横向负载转移率降低44%、圈速提升8.7%、峰值横向加速度提升21.3%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轮式四足机器人的自主竞速中，高速过弯时横向负载转移会导致稳定性下降甚至翻车，传统控制方法难以同时兼顾速度和稳定性，因此需要一种能够主动控制侧倾的框架来提升竞速性能和安全性。

Method: 提出分层控制框架：离线生成最优赛道线，在线MPC规划器基于车辆动力学自行车模型最小化横向负载转移率(LTR)，底层使用强化学习(RL)策略直接控制16个执行器，利用腿部关节作为主动悬架产生抗侧倾力矩。

Result: 物理赛道实验表明：主动侧倾控制使平均LTR降低44%，最快圈速提升8.7%，峰值横向加速度提升21.3%达到1.98 m/s²，且在超出非倾斜基线控制器范围的高速下保持稳健稳定性。

Conclusion: 结合MPC和RL的分层侧倾控制框架能有效提升轮式四足机器人在自主竞速中的性能和稳定性，该方法在高速场景下显著优于传统非倾斜控制方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a hierarchical control framework using model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) for active roll control to manage lateral load transfer during autonomous racing of a wheeled quadruped. The framework integrates offline time-optimal raceline generation, an online MPC planner that actively minimizes the lateral Load Transfer Ratio (LTR), and a low-level, whole-body RL policy deployed directly onto the robot's 16 actuators. The MPC is based on a vehicle dynamics bicycle model of the Unitree Go2-W platform. The robot's leg actuators act as active suspension where knee joints generate anti-roll torque to bank into turns. Physical track experiments demonstrate that active roll control reduces mean LTR by up to 44%, improves the fastest lap time by 8.7%, and boosts peak lateral acceleration capability by 21.3% to 1.98 $m/s^2$, maintaining robust high-speed stability beyond the range of a non-tilting baseline controller. Supplementary code and video can be found at https://github.com/meisman-ucb/go2w-roll-control-mpc

</details>


### [179] [Layered Outer-Loop Control for Disturbance-Robust Multi-Waypoint UAV Arrival](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26315)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层终端控制架构用于无人机位置调节，并通过PyBullet、PX4/Gazebo和硬件三个阶段的评估，验证了将控制器核心设计与基准特定优化分离的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机位置控制在仿真中容易实现，但在实际部署中难以兼顾快速接近、目标附近良好行为以及跨基准的可靠性。现有方法往往过度依赖单一基准，缺乏对真实闭环动力学的考量。

Method: 提出分层终端控制架构：Phase I在PyBullet中快速选择控制器核心（平滑接近生成、持续偏差补偿、监督式终端调节）；Phase II将架构迁移至PX4/Gazebo闭环系统，暴露仿真优势在真实部署中的失效点；最终在Vicon跟踪的Tello硬件上完成验证。

Result: Phase I中控制器达到0.024m的平均晚期风扰动误差。通过迁移导向的选择规则，最终控制器在更严苛的闭环评估中保持稳健。Grace回溯分析显示部分失败源于完成语义而非路径点缺失。

Conclusion: 当将控制器核心设计与基准特定优化分离，并在更难的闭环评估下仍可辩护时，基准成功才更具信息量。

Abstract: Disturbance-robust UAV position control is easy to demonstrate in benign simulations but much harder to make fast in approach, well behaved near the target, and credible beyond a single benchmark. This letter presents a layered terminal-control architecture for multi-waypoint UAV position regulation together with a staged evaluation across PyBullet, PX4/Gazebo, and hardware. Phase I uses a PyBullet benchmark with stochastic wind for rapid structural selection, identifying a controller core that separates smooth approach generation, persistent-bias compensation, and supervised near-target terminal regulation. Phase II carries only that main architecture into a more demanding PX4/Gazebo closed loop, where the outer-loop controller acts through a cascaded flight stack with delay-sensitive settling and stronger transit-to-hover coupling. This step exposes which benchmark gains survive autopilot-mediated dynamics and which refinements collapse once the loop becomes more deployment-like. In Phase I, the bare controller attains 0.024 m mean late-stage wind error. In Phase II, the final controller is selected using a transfer-oriented rule emphasizing absence of benchmark priors, cross-scenario balance, and deployable supervisory logic. Strict is used as the primary reporting reference; the supplementary retrospective Grace analysis shows that part of the residual failure set is sensitive to completion semantics rather than gross waypoint-miss behaviour. The evaluation is completed on one Vicon-tracked Tello stack through a two-level hardware study. Taken together, the results suggest that benchmark success becomes more informative when the main controller design is separated from benchmark-specific refinement and remains defensible under harder closed-loop evaluation.

</details>


### [180] [KRVF: A Source-Aware Semantic Voxel World Representation for Edge Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26321)
*Runfeng Ling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KRVF是一种面向边缘移动操作的语义体素世界表示方法，它通过任务导向的体素编码（包含占用、颜色、语义证据、时间新鲜度和证据来源）来构建局部世界状态，并实现了深度故障感知的目标推理、地图-感知反馈循环修复以及任务级查询操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人需要当前、可查询、语义有意义且能在边缘计算约束下使用的世界模型，而现有的重建导向建图管线主要优化全局几何保真度，不适合任务导向的移动操作场景。

Method: KRVF采用源感知的语义体素世界表示，将测量占用与语义先验假设分离，实现深度故障感知的目标推理；通过渲染地图先验深度进行修复，关闭建图与感知之间的反馈循环；并提供任务级查询算子用于语义对象和抓取候选。

Result: 在ROS 2中实现了KRVF表示，将在线RGB-D观测转化为任务导向的机器人记忆。

Conclusion: KRVF为边缘移动操作提供了一种源感知的语义体素世界表示，实现了从传统几何重建到任务导向建图的范式转变。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators need world models that are current, queryable, semantically meaningful, and usable under edge-compute constraints. This technical report presents KRVF, a source-aware semantic voxel world representation for edge mobile manipulation. Unlike reconstruction-centric mapping pipelines that primarily optimize global geometric fidelity, KRVF represents local world state as task-oriented voxels that encode occupancy, color, semantic evidence, temporal freshness, and evidence source. The representation separates measured occupancy from semantic-prior hypotheses, enabling depth-failure-aware object reasoning without silently corrupting persistent geometry. KRVF also closes a feedback loop between mapping and sensing by rendering map-prior depth for repair, and exposes task-level query operators for semantic objects and grasp candidates. The report formalizes the KRVF representation and documents a ROS 2 implementation that turns online RGB-D observations into a task-facing robot memory.

</details>


### [181] [Scaling Nonlinear Optimization: Many Problems One GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26341)
*John Viljoen,Johanna Haffner,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了jaxipm，这是首个基于JAX实现的GPU批处理NLP求解器，通过异构迭代融合和迭代级批处理技术，在四旋翼无人机非线性MPC任务中实现了相比IPOPT最高32.85倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NLP求解器（如IPOPT）在CPU上运行且一次只能求解单个问题，无法集成到GPU批处理学习管线中；而采样方法（如强化学习）因能利用GPU批处理模拟器而成为主流。需要一种GPU批处理NLP求解器来弥合这一差距。

Method: 基于IPOPT算法，在JAX中重新设计实现。核心技术包括：（1）异构迭代融合（heterogeneous iteration fusion）——消除控制流；（2）迭代级批处理（iteration level batching）——最小化GPU空闲时间。

Result: 在四旋翼无人机非线性MPC基准测试中（含障碍物参考跟踪、多机无碰撞导航、密集环境导航），相比IPOPT实现了最高32.85倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: jaxipm成功将NLP求解器带入GPU批处理时代，为机器人学中约束优化问题的快速求解开辟了新路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Many robotics problems, including trajectory optimization, inverse kinematics, and contact-rich motion planning, reduce to nonlinear programs (NLPs). Mature NLP solvers such as IPOPT can solve these problems, offering hard constraint satisfaction, optimality guarantees, and favorable scaling with problem dimension. These solvers underpin gradient-based methods in robotics, yet remain CPU-bound and solve only one problem at a time, preventing their integration into GPU-batched learning pipelines. On the other hand, sampling-based approaches such as reinforcement learning, model predictive path integral, and imitation learning have become the core of modern robotics research due to their ability to leverage GPU-batched simulators. These simulators can generate orders of magnitude more dynamics rollouts per second than was previously possible.
